	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;text-align:center;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">PROPOSAL&#x00a0;1<BR>ELECTION OF DIRECTORS<BR></FONT><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-1" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">(Item&#x00a0;1 on the Proxy Card)</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Background</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Under our articles of association, the number of directors on our Board of Directors shall be no less than five and no more than 11. Our Board of Directors is currently comprised of ten directors: Mrs.&#x00a0;Lilach Asher Topilsky, Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer, Mr.&#x00a0;Ishay Davidi, Ms. Karnit Goldwasser, Ms. Lilach Payorski, Mr.&#x00a0;Leon Recanati, Prof. Ari Shamiss, Mr.&#x00a0;David Tsur, all of whom were elected to serve in such capacity at our 2022 annual general meeting of shareholders, Prof. Benjamin Dekel and Mr.&#x00a0;Assaf Itshayek, who were elected to serve as external directors, within the meaning of the Companies Law, each for a three year term, at our extraordinary general meeting of shareholders held on August&#x00a0;29, 2023. Each of our directors (other than our external directors) generally holds office until the first annual general meeting of shareholders following his or her appointment (unless the tenure of such director expires earlier, or a director is removed from office pursuant to the Israeli Companies Law).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Each of our currently serving directors other than Prof. Ari Shamiss (excluding our external directors) is standing for re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-election</FONT> at the Meeting, to hold office until our next annual general meeting of shareholders and until his or her successor is elected and qualified, subject to our articles of association and applicable law. Our Board of Directors has affirmatively determined that each of Mrs.&#x00a0;Lilach Asher Topilsky, Mr.&#x00a0;Uri Botzer, Mr.&#x00a0;Ishay Davidi, Ms. Karnit Goldwasser, Ms. Lilach Payorski, Mr.&#x00a0;Leon Recanati, Mr.&#x00a0;David Tsur, Prof. Benjamin Dekel and Mr.&#x00a0;Assaf Itshayek is an &#x201c;independent director&#x201d; as defined under NASDAQ Listing Rules. Accordingly, subject to shareholder approval of the above director nominees, our Board of Directors will consist of nine members, all of whom satisfy the independence requirements of the NASDAQ Listing Rules.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In accordance with the Israeli Companies Law, each of the director nominees has certified to us that he or she meets all the requirements of the Israeli Companies Law for election as a director of a public company, possesses the necessary qualifications and is able to dedicate sufficient time to fulfill his or her duties as a director of the Company, taking into consideration our company&#x2019;s size and special needs.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Nominees for Director</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The following biographical information is provided with respect to each director nominee based upon our records and information provided to us by each nominee.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Lilach Asher Topilsky</FONT> has served as a member of our board of directors since December&#x00a0;2019, as the Chair of our board of directors since August&#x00a0;2020, and serves as a member of our Compensation Committee and Strategy Committee. Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky has been a Senior Partner in the FIMI Opportunity Funds, Israel&#x2019;s largest group of private equity funds, since December&#x00a0;2019. Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky currently serves as the chairman of G1 Security Systems Ltd. (TASE), Rimoni Industries Ltd. (TASE), SOS Ltd. Elyakim Ben Ari Group Ltd. and Amal and beyond Ltd. and as a director at Amiad Water Systems Ltd. (AIM), Ashot Ashkelon Industries Ltd. (TASE) and Tel Aviv University. Prior to joining FIMI, Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky served as the President and CEO of Israel Discount Bank (TASE), one of the leading banking groups in Israel, as the Chairman at IDBNY BANKCORP and as a director at IDB Bank New&#x00a0;York from 2014&#x00a0;&#x2013;&#x00a0;2019. Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky also served as the Chairman of Mercantile Bank from&#x00a0;2014&#x00a0;&#x2013;&#x00a0;2016. Before that, Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky served as a member of the management of Bank Hapoalim (TASE) as Deputy CEO&#x00a0;&amp; Head of Retail Banking Division (2009&#x00a0;&#x2013;&#x00a0;2013)&#x00a0;&amp; Head of Strategy&#x00a0;&amp; Planning Division (2007&#x00a0;&#x2013;&#x00a0;2009). Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky served as a Strategy Consultant at The Boston Consulting Group (BCG, Chicago&#x00a0;1997&#x00a0;&#x2013;&#x00a0;1998) and at Shaldor Strategy Consulting (Israel&#x00a0;1995&#x00a0;&#x2013;&#x00a0;1996). Mrs.&#x00a0;Asher Topilsky holds an M.B.A. degree from Kellogg School of Management, Northwestern University, Chicago, USA (1997), and a B.A. degree in Management and Economics from Tel Aviv University, Israel (Magna Cum Laude, 1994).</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Uri Botzer</FONT> has served as a member of our board of directors since December&#x00a0;2022. Mr.&#x00a0;Botzer has been a Junior Partner in the FIMI Opportunity Funds, Israel&#x2019;s largest group of private equity funds, since 2019. Prior to joining FIMI, Mr.&#x00a0;Botzer served as a lawyer at FISCHER (FBC&#x00a0;&amp; Co.). Mr.&#x00a0;Botzer holds a B.A. degree in Business Administration and a LL.B. (Bachelor of Law), Cum Laude, from Reichman University, Herzliya.</P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">9</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Ishay Davidi</FONT> has served on our board of directors since December&#x00a0;2019. Mr.&#x00a0;Davidi is the Founder and has served as Chief Executive Officer of the FIMI Opportunity Funds, Israel&#x2019;s largest group of private equity funds, since 1996. Mr.&#x00a0;Davidi currently serves as the Chairman of the Board of Directors of Polyram Plastic Industries Ltd (TASE) and Ashot Ashkelon Industries Ltd. (TASE). Mr.&#x00a0;Davidi also serves as a director of Bet Shemesh Engines Ltd. (TASE), C.&#x00a0;Mer Industries Ltd. (TASE), G1 Security Systems Ltd. (TASE), PCB Technologies Ltd. (TASE), Rekah Pharmaceutical Industries (TASE), SOS Ltd., GreenStream Ltd., Amiad Water Systems Ltd (AIM), Rimoni Industries Ltd. (TASE), Elyakim Ben<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Ari</FONT> Group Ltd. and Amal and beyond Ltd. Mr.&#x00a0;Davidi previously served as the Chairman of the board of directors of Infinya Ltd. (TASE), Inrom, Retalix (previously traded on NASDAQ and TASE) and Tefron Ltd. (NYSE and TASE) and as a director of Gilat Satellite Networks Ltd. (NASDAQ and TASE), Pharm Up Ltd (TASE), Ham<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Let</FONT> Ltd. (TASE), Ormat Industries Ltd. (previously traded on TASE), Lipman Electronic Engineering Ltd. From December&#x00a0;2009 to December&#x00a0;2012, Ms. Payorski served as Head of Finance at PMC<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Sierra</FONT> (NASDAQ:&#x00a0;PMCS), a company operating in the semiconductors industry, which was subsequently acquired by Microsemi Corporation. (NASDAQ:&#x00a0;ODD) and Scodix Ltd. (TASE:&#x00a0;SCDX). Ms. Payorski holds a B.A. degree in Accounting and Economics from Tel Aviv University. Ms. Payorski also completed the Board of Directors and Senior Corporate Officers Program at LAHAV, School of Management, Tel Aviv University.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">Leon Recanati</FONT> has served on our board of directors since May&#x00a0;2005, as the Chairman of our board of directors from March&#x00a0;2013 to August&#x00a0;2020, and served as the Chairman of our Compensation Committee from February&#x00a0;2019 until September&#x00a0;2023. 		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">Azorim Investment Development and Construction Co Ltd., Delek Israel Fuel Corporation and Super<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-Sol</FONT> Ltd. 		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="BoldItalic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:bold;">David Tsur</FONT> has served on our board of directors since our inception and serves as a member of our Strategy Committee. Mr.&#x00a0;Tsur served as the Active Deputy Chairman on a half<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-time</FONT> basis from July&#x00a0;2015 until December&#x00a0;31, 2019. Mr.&#x00a0;Tsur served as our Chief Executive Officer from our inception until July&#x00a0;2015. Mr.&#x00a0;Tsur currently serves on the Board of Directors of Kanabo Ltd. (LSE) and as a director of BioHarvest Sciences Inc. (CSE). Prior to co<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-founding</FONT> Kamada in 1990, Mr.&#x00a0;Tsur served as Chief Executive Officer of Arad Systems and RAD Chemicals Inc. Mr.&#x00a0;Tsur holds a B.A. degree in Economics and International Relations and an M.B.A. degree in Business Management, both from the Hebrew University of Jerusalem.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">As permitted by the NASDAQ Listing Rules, we follow Israeli law and practice rather than the NASDAQ requirement for independent director oversight over our director nominations process. In accordance with Israeli law and practice, directors are recommended by our Board of Directors for election by our shareholders. Our Board of Directors reviewed the qualifications and suitability of each of the director nominees and our Board of Directors as a whole. We believe that our Board of Directors contains highly qualified and talented directors, including directors with global biopharmaceutical and financial experience. Accordingly, our Board of Directors approved the nomination of each of the director nominees.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Under a shareholders&#x2019; agreement entered into on March&#x00a0;6, 2013, the Recanati Group, on the one hand, and the Damar Group, on the other hand, have each agreed to vote the ordinary shares beneficially owned by them in favor of the election of director nominees designated by the other group as follows: (i)&#x00a0;three director nominees, so long as the other group beneficially owns at least 7.5% of our outstanding share capital, (ii)&#x00a0;two director nominees, so long as the other group beneficially owns at least 5.0% (but less than 7.5%) of our outstanding share capital, and (iii)&#x00a0;one director nominee, so long as the other group beneficially owns at least 2.5% (but less than 5.0%) of our outstanding share capital. In addition, to the extent that after the designation of the foregoing director nominees there are additional director vacancies, each of the Recanati Group and Damar Group have agreed to vote the ordinary shares beneficially owned by them in favor of such additional director nominees designated by the party who beneficially owns the larger voting rights in our company. We are not aware of any reason why the nominees, if elected, would be unable or unwilling to serve as directors. Should the nominees be unavailable for election, the proxies will be voted for substitute nominees designated by our Board of Directors.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">If elected at the Meeting, the director nominees will be paid an annual fee and per<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-meeting</FONT> fees in the maximum amounts payable from time to time by us under the Second and Third Addendums to the Companies Regulations (Rules Regarding Compensation and Expense Reimbursement of External Directors), 2000. In addition, if elected at the Meeting, the director nominees shall continue to benefit from indemnification and exculpation agreements previously entered into with each of them, as well as from directors&#x2019; and officers&#x2019; liability insurance as we shall procure from time to time.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Proposal</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The shareholders are being asked to re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-elect</FONT> the seven directors named above to serve as members of our Board of Directors until our next annual general meeting of shareholders and until their respective successors are duly elected by the shareholders of the Company. Each director nominee shall be voted on separately.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Approval Required</FONT></P>

		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Board Recommendation</FONT></P>

	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="H1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:1;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">PROPOSAL&#x00a0;2<BR>APPROVAL OF AN INCREASE IN BASE SALARY OF OUR CHIEF EXECUTIVE OFFICER<BR></FONT><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-1" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">(Item&#x00a0;2 on the Proxy Card)</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Background</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Under the Israeli Companies Law, the payment of compensation to a chief executive officer that is consistent with a company&#x2019;s compensation policy must be approved by the compensation committee, board of directors and shareholders by the Special Majority (see above &#x201c;Vote Required for Approval of the Proposals&#x201d;), in that order.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Mr.&#x00a0;Amir London has served as our Chief Executive Officer since July&#x00a0;2015. Prior to that, Mr.&#x00a0;London served as our Senior Vice President, Business Development from December&#x00a0;2013. Mr.&#x00a0;London brings with him over 25&#x00a0;years of senior management and international business development experience. From 2011 to 2013, Mr.&#x00a0;London served as the Chief Operating Officer of Fidelis Diagnostics, a U.S.<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> provider of innovative in<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-office</FONT> medical diagnostic services. Earlier in his career, from 2009 to 2011, Mr.&#x00a0;London served as the Chief Executive Officer of Promedico, an Israeli<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-based</FONT> $350&#x00a0;million healthcare distribution company, and from 2006 to 2009, he served as the General Manager of Cure Medical, providing contract manufacturing services for clinical studies, as well as home<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-care</FONT> solutions. From 1995 to 2006, Mr.&#x00a0;London was a Partner with Tefen, an international publicly traded operations management consulting firm, responsible for the firm&#x2019;s global biopharma practice. Mr.&#x00a0;London holds a B.Sc. degree in Industrial and Management Engineering from the Technion&#x00a0;&#x2014;&#x00a0;Israel Institute of Technology.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors conducted an annual review of the compensation of Mr.&#x00a0;London in accordance with our existing Compensation Policy for Executive Officers (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Compensation Policy</FONT>&#x201d;) and taking into consideration current market trends in executive compensation as previously provided by an independent compensation consultant to ensure that his compensation components and total compensation remain aligned with the companies that the Company competes with for human capital and positioned at the appropriate benchmark compensation. Mr.&#x00a0;London&#x2019;s base salary was last increased, effective as of July&#x00a0;1, 2022, to a gross monthly salary of NIS 96,000 (approximately $24,950), pursuant to the approval of our shareholders at our 2019 annual general meeting of shareholders and subsequent immaterial increases by our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors based on an exemption from shareholder approval under Israeli law. Based on such review, our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors concluded that Mr.&#x00a0;London&#x2019;s gross monthly salary should be increased to reflect his performance and significant contribution to our company, as well as to reflect best market practices and his peer group. Accordingly, our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors concluded that, subject to shareholder approval, Mr.&#x00a0;London&#x2019;s gross monthly salary should be increased by 4.17% to a gross monthly salary of NIS 100,000 (approximately $25,990), effective as of July&#x00a0;1, 2023. Other than as described above, Mr.&#x00a0;London&#x2019;s terms of engagement as Chief Executive Officer shall remain as currently in effect. Each of our Compensation Committee and Board of Directors determined that the proposed increased salary is consistent with our Compensation Policy.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Proposed Resolution</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">It is proposed that at the Meeting the following resolution be adopted:</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-left:24pt;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">&#x201c;RESOLVED, to approve the increase in the base salary of Mr.&#x00a0;Amir London, the Company&#x2019;s Chief Executive Officer, as set forth in the Proxy Statement for the Meeting.&#x201d;</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Approval Required</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-left:24pt;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">See &#x201c;Vote Required for Approval of the Proposals&#x201d; above.</P>

The Company fulfills these obligations by filing reports with the SEC.&#x00a0;Our filings with the SEC are available to the public on the SEC&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.sec.gov</FONT>, the ISA&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.magna.isa.gov.il</FONT>, the TASE&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">http:</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">//</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">maya.tase.co.il</FONT> and under the &#x201c;Investor&#x00a0;&amp; Media&#x201d; portion of our website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.kamada.com</FONT>. The contents of our website do not form part of the proxy solicitation material.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">As a foreign private issuer, we are exempt from the rules under the Exchange&#x00a0;Act related to the furnishing and content of proxy statements. The circulation of this proxy statement and related notice should not be taken as an admission that we are subject to those proxy rules.</P>

	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">

		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:0pt;text-align:center;margin-top:0pt;"><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-1" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:normal;">2 Holzman Street<BR>Weizmann Science Park<BR>P.O.&#x00a0;Box 4081<BR>Rehovot 7670402, Israel</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;text-align:center;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">NOTICE OF ANNUAL GENERAL MEETING OF SHAREHOLDERS</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_flush" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Dear Shareholder:</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">We cordially invite you to attend the Annual General Meeting of Shareholders of Kamada Ltd. (the &#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Meeting</FONT>&#x201d;) to be held at our offices at 2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel, on Thursday, December&#x00a0;28, 2023, at 4:00&#x00a0;p.m. (Israel time), for the following purposes:</P>
		<P CLASS="NL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">1.<FONT STYLE="width: 20px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>To re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-elect</FONT> seven directors to serve as members of our Board of Directors until our next annual general meeting of shareholders and until their respective successors are duly elected by the shareholders of the Company.</P>
		<P CLASS="NL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">3.<FONT STYLE="width: 20px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>To approve an increase in the base salary of Mr.&#x00a0;Amir London, our Chief Executive Officer.</P>
		<P CLASS="NL_m" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:48pt;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:-24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">7.<FONT STYLE="width: 20px;display: inline-block;">&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;&#x00a0;</FONT>To ratify and approve the re<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-appointment</FONT> of Kost Forer Gabbay&#x00a0;&amp; Kasierer, a member of Ernst&#x00a0;&amp; Young Global, as our independent registered public accountants for the year ending December&#x00a0;31, 2023 and for such additional period until our next annual general meeting of shareholders.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In addition, at the Meeting, representatives of our management will be available to review and discuss our consolidated financial statements for the year ended December&#x00a0;31, 2022.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Our Board of Directors recommends a vote &#x201c;FOR&#x201d; the election of each of the director nominees set forth in Proposal&#x00a0;1 and each of the other proposals that are described in the attached Proxy Statement.</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Shareholders of record at the close of business on November&#x00a0;20, 2023, are entitled to notice of and to vote at the Meeting. You can vote either by mailing in your proxy or in person by attending the Meeting. If voting by proxy, we will generally not be able to include your vote in the tally of ordinary shares voted at the Meeting unless your proxy is received by our transfer agent or at our registered office in Israel at least 48&#x00a0;hours prior to the appointed time of the Meeting. If you attend the Meeting, you may revoke your proxy (if previously submitted) and vote in person. If you are a beneficial owner of shares registered in the name of your broker, bank, trustee or nominee and you wish to vote in person at the Meeting, you must first obtain a &#x201c;legal proxy&#x201d; from your broker, bank, trustee or nominee that holds your shares giving you the right to vote the shares at the Meeting. Detailed proxy voting instructions are provided both in the Proxy Statement and on the enclosed proxy card.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">If you are a beneficial owner of shares registered in the name of a member of the Tel Aviv Stock Exchange (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">TASE</FONT>&#x201d;) and wish to vote, either by proxy or in person by attending the Meeting, you must deliver to us a proof of ownership in accordance with the Israeli Companies Law, 1999 (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Israeli Companies Law</FONT>&#x201d;) and the Israeli Companies Regulations (Proof of Ownership of Shares for Voting at General Meetings), 2000. Such certification may be obtained at the TASE member&#x2019;s offices or may be sent to the shareholder by mail (subject to payment of the cost of mailing), at the election of the shareholder, provided that the shareholder&#x2019;s request is submitted with respect to a specific securities account. Shareholders who hold shares through members of the TASE may also vote electronically via the electronic voting system of the Israel Securities Authority (&#x201c;<FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">ISA</FONT>&#x201d;) up to six&#x00a0;hours before the time fixed for the Meeting. You should receive instructions about electronic voting from the TASE member through which you hold your shares.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Quorum</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The presence (in person, by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system) of two or more shareholders holding or representing, in the aggregate, at least twenty<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-five</FONT> percent of our Company&#x2019;s voting rights will constitute a quorum at the Meeting. No business will be considered or determined at the Meeting unless the requisite quorum is present within half an hour from the time designated for the Meeting. If within half an hour from the time designated for the Meeting a quorum is not present, the Meeting will stand adjourned to the same&#x00a0;day in the following week, at the same time </P>
		</DIV>

		<P CLASS="RRH" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:0;orphans:1;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:center;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin: 10px 0px 10px 0pt; page-break-after: always; border-bottom: 1px solid; margin-top: 10.0pt; margin-right: 0in; margin-bottom: 10.0pt; margin-left: 0in; text-align: center; font-size: 10.0pt; font-family: TimesNewRomanPSStd-Regular; color: black;">1</P>



	<DIV CLASS="_idGenObjectStyleOverride-1" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;border-width:0pt;">
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;text-indent:0pt;margin-top:8pt;">and place. Any number of shareholders present (in person or by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system) will constitute a quorum at the adjourned meeting. This notice will serve as notice of such reconvened meeting if no quorum is present at the original date and time and no further notice of the reconvened meeting will be given to shareholders.</P>
		<P CLASS="H2" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:bold;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:12pt;orphans:99;page-break-after:avoid;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:0;widows:1;margin-top:12pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Vote Required for Approval of the Proposals</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Each ordinary share entitles the holder to one vote.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">The affirmative vote of the holders of a majority of the ordinary shares represented at the Meeting, in person, by proxy or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system, entitled to vote and voting on the matter, is required to elect each of the director nominees set forth in Proposal1 and to approve each of the other proposals.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">In addition to the foregoing majority requirement, the approval of Proposal&#x00a0;2 is also subject to the fulfillment of one of the following additional voting requirements: (i)&#x00a0;the shares voting in favor of the proposal (excluding abstentions) include at least a majority of the shares voted by shareholders who are not controlling shareholders and shareholders who do not have a personal interest in the proposal, or (ii)&#x00a0;the total number of shares voted against the proposal by shareholders who are not controlling shareholders and shareholders who do not have a personal interest in the proposal does not exceed two<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-percent</FONT> (2%) of our outstanding voting rights.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Other than the FIMI Funds, we are unaware of any other shareholder that would be deemed to be a controlling shareholder of the Company (within the meaning of Israeli law) for purposes of the calculation of the Special Majority. <FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">A shareholder who signs and returns a proxy card will be deemed to be confirming that such shareholder, and any related party of such shareholder, is not a controlling shareholder for purposes of Proposal&#x00a0;2. If you believe that you, or a related party of yours, may be deemed to be a controlling shareholder and you wish to participate in the vote on Proposal&#x00a0;2, you should contact Mr.&#x00a0;Nir Livneh, our Vice President General Counsel and Corporate Secretary (Tel: +972</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">-733-321705</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">, nirl@kamada.com) or Ms. Rotem Green, our Legal Counsel (Tel: +972</FONT><FONT CLASS="nobreak"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">-733-366186</FONT></FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">, </FONT><FONT CLASS="CharOverride-3" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">rotemg@kamada.com</FONT><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">).</FONT></P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;"><FONT CLASS="Bold" STYLE="font-style:normal;font-weight:bold;">Each shareholder voting on Proposal&#x00a0;2 is required to indicate on the proxy card or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic voting system, or, if voting in person at the Meeting, inform us prior to voting on the matter at the Meeting, whether or not the shareholder has a personal interest in Proposal&#x00a0;2. Otherwise, the shareholder&#x2019;s vote will not be counted for the purposes of the proposal. </FONT>Under the Israeli Companies Law, a &#x201c;personal interest&#x201d; of a shareholder in an act or transaction of a company (i)&#x00a0;includes a personal interest of (a)&#x00a0;any spouse, sibling, parent, grandparent or descendant of the shareholder, any descendant, sibling or parent of a spouse of the shareholder and the spouse of any of the foregoing; and (b)&#x00a0;a company with respect to which the shareholder (or any of the foregoing relatives of the shareholder) serves as a director or chief executive officer, owns at least 5% of the outstanding shares or voting rights or has the right to appoint one or more directors or the chief executive officer; and (ii)&#x00a0;excludes a personal interest arising solely from the ownership of shares. Under the Israeli Companies Law, in the case of a person voting by proxy, &#x201c;personal interest&#x201d; includes the personal interest of either the proxy holder or the shareholder granting the proxy, whether or not the proxy holder has discretion how to vote.</P>
		<P CLASS="Text_ind" STYLE="margin:0;padding:0;border-width:0;font-family:Times New Roman PS Std, serif;font-size:10pt;font-style:normal;font-variant:normal;font-weight:normal;margin-bottom:0;margin-left:0;margin-right:0;margin-top:8pt;orphans:2;page-break-after:auto;page-break-before:auto;text-align:justify;text-indent:24pt;widows:3;margin-top:8pt;">Shareholders may review the full version of the proposed resolutions in the Proxy Statement as well as the accompanying proxy card, via the website of the U.S.&#x00a0;Securities and Exchange Commission at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.sec.gov </FONT>or via the ISA&#x2019;s electronic filing system at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">http:</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">//</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">www.magna.isa.gov.il</FONT> or the TASE&#x2019;s website at <FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">http:</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">//</FONT><FONT CLASS="Italic" STYLE="font-style:italic;font-weight:normal;">maya.tase.co.il</FONT>, and also at our offices upon prior notice and during regular business&#x00a0;hours (2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel; Tel: +972<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-8-9406472</FONT>) until the date of the Meeting. Our Company&#x2019;s representatives are Mr.&#x00a0;Nir Livneh, our Vice President General Counsel and Corporate Secretary and Ms. Rotem Green, our Legal Counsel (2 Holzman Street, Weizmann Science Park, Rehovot, Israel; Tel: +972<FONT CLASS="nobreak">-733-321705</FONT>).</P>
